Abstract: We reconsider the Bardeen black hole surrounded by a Kiselev fluid, obtained by adding a Kiselev term to the Ayon-Beato-Garcia-Bardeen metric. Contrary to claims in the literature, the solution is regular at the origin only for $c=0$: for $c\neq0$ curvature invariants diverge as $r\to0$, and the spacetime is asymptotically de Sitter-like rather than flat, with a cosmological horizon $r_c$ for $0<c<c_N(q)$. We map the full $(q,c)$ domain of horizon structures in closed form, locating the extremal and Nariai-type boundaries and their cusp. Using the Ma-Zhao prescription for the mass-dependent nonlinear-electrodynamics source, we derive the corrected entropy and verify the extended first law and Smarr relation to machine precision by computer-algebra differentiation, not through the entropy's own definition. The heat capacity shows a single Davies-type divergence, located as a closed-form quartic and expanded analytically in $c$. In thermodynamic geometry, the Quevedo-metric curvature has a spurious second singularity generated by the entropy's integration constant and the non-uniqueness of its conformal factor; we isolate this artefact and show only the Davies-point divergence is physical. The Ruppeiner curvature, identically zero for pure Bardeen, becomes non-zero once $c\neq0$. For the shadow, we compute the critical impact parameter $b_c(q,c,w)$ and show the nominal bound $c\lesssim10^{-2}/M$ from the EHT image of Sagittarius A* is not self-consistent: the same $c$ places the cosmological horizon nine orders of magnitude closer than Earth's distance to Sgr A*, and the bound is fourteen orders of magnitude larger than $H_0/c$, the true cosmological dark-energy scale. We reinterpret the EHT constraint as bounding $c$ only near the horizon, not as an astrophysical or cosmological bound on the quintessence parameter.

</details>


### [108] [Four-derivative corrections to the neutral-scalar Christodoulou threshold in charged de Sitter black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03805)
*Reggie C. Pantig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维带电de Sitter黑洞中中性无质量标量场在Einstein-Maxwell有效理论下，受领头宇称偶四导数修正影响的Christodoulou正则性判据；推导了修正后的静态解、冷极值关系、近视界几何、柯西视界表面引力及标量拟正规模谱；发现冷极限下s波正则性比在首阶保持不变，但高角动量模受喉部半径形变影响；Christodoulou边界因外区标量谱与不等视界表面引力而发生位移；宇宙学尺度变化导致阈值移动方向反转，并伴随拟正规模谱隙主导族的转变；极值质量参数、冷喉部结构与中性标量边界由不同有效系数控制，故极值性不决定中性标量正则性阈值修正的普适方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能修正（如量子引力效应）如何影响黑洞柯西视界附近的经典正则性判据，特别是Christodoulou判据在de Sitter背景下对中性‘旁观者’标量场的鲁棒性；探究有效场论中高阶导数项对黑洞热力学、近视界几何与动力学谱的耦合效应。

Method: 在Einstein-Maxwell有效理论中引入领头宇称偶四导数修正项（如R², R_{μν}R^{μν}, F⁴型），求解修正后的静态Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter黑洞解；解析计算冷极值极限下的近视界AdS₂×S²喉部几何、Cauchy视界表面引力κ₋；分离变量并求解中性标量场波动方程，在冷极限下将其映射至有限温度AdS₂上的二维问题；利用拟正规模谱间隙（spectral gap）定义Christodoulou正则性阈值，并分析其对有效系数和宇宙学常数Λ的依赖。

Result: 1) 冷极限下s波模式的正则性比Γ = κ₊/|κ₋|在首阶修正下保持不变；2) 高ℓ模响应于内外喉部半径之比的形变；3) Christodoulou边界随Λ变化呈现非单调位移：小/中Λ时正系数使阈值向更大视界间距移动，大Λ冷支上则反向移动；4) 该反转对应主导拟正规模谱隙的家族切换（如从s波到p波）；5) 控制极值质量M_ext、冷喉部结构及Christodoulou边界的三组有效系数彼此独立。

Conclusion: 中性标量场的Cauchy视界正则性对高阶引力修正的响应高度依赖于宇宙学尺度Λ与拟正规模谱家族竞争；不存在由黑洞极值性唯一决定的普适修正方向；必须联合考量有效重力理论的系数结构、背景几何的尺度分层以及动力学谱的离散性，才能一致刻画极端黑洞中旁观者场的强引力正则性。

Abstract: We study leading parity-even four-derivative corrections to the linear Christodoulou regularity criterion for a neutral, massless, minimally coupled spectator scalar in four-dimensional charged de Sitter black holes. We derive the corrected static solution, cold extremality relation, near-horizon geometry, Cauchy-horizon surface gravity, and neutral scalar quasinormal spectrum within Einstein-Maxwell effective theory. Near cold extremality, the scalar equation reduces analytically to the finite-temperature anti-de Sitter two-dimensional throat problem. We find that the dominant nonstationary spherically symmetric mode retains its cold-limit regularity ratio through first order, while higher angular sectors respond to the deformation of the relative throat radii. The finite-distance neutral-scalar Christodoulou boundary nevertheless shifts because the relevant exterior scalar spectrum and the unequal horizon surface gravities enter the regularity ratio. At smaller and intermediate cosmological scales, positive effective coefficients move the Christodoulou threshold to larger normalized separation of the Cauchy and event horizons. Toward the larger cosmological-scale part of the cold branch, the same coefficient directions move that threshold back toward the cold endpoint. The reversal accompanies a transition in the quasinormal family that supplies the spectral gap. We further show that the coefficient combination governing the fixed-charge extremal mass parameter differs generically from those governing the cold throat and the finite-distance neutral-scalar boundary. Extremality therefore does not determine a universal direction for the neutral-scalar Christodoulou-threshold correction. Our results identify cosmological scale and spectral-family competition as essential elements in relating effective gravity, black-hole extremality, and Cauchy-horizon regularity in this spectator sector.

</details>


### [109] [Optical Signatures of Black Holes Surrounded by a Generalized Cloud of Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04173)
*Marcos V. de S. Silva,Ednaldo L. B. Junior,Carlos F. S. Pereira,Diego Sáez-Chillón Gómez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义弦云环绕黑洞（Letelier-Alencar时空）的光学特征，分析了类光测地线、光子球半径、临界撞击参数、阴影半径约束、弱场下光线偏折角（使用Gauss-Bonnet方法和测地线微扰展开）、Shapiro时延、以及薄吸积盘的成像特性；发现弦参数可双向调制光子球与阴影尺寸，非渐近平直性导致阴影半径与临界撞击参数不等价，并揭示不同光学观测量探测光子传播的不同几何层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非渐近平直、含拓扑缺陷（广义弦云）的黑洞时空对关键光学可观测量（阴影、偏折、时延、成像）的影响，以区分其与标准广义相对论预言的差异，并为EHT等观测提供理论约束。

Method: 解析求解Letelier-Alencar时空中的类光测地线；计算光子球半径与临界撞击参数；结合Sgr A*与M87*的阴影观测上限推导弦参数约束；采用Gauss-Bonnet定理与测地线微扰法双重验证弱场偏折角；解析计算Shapiro时延中渐近锥形背景贡献；通过后向光线追踪模拟薄盘成像并分析转移函数与爱因斯坦环位移。

Result: 弦参数可使光子球半径与临界撞击参数相对于Schwarzschild值增大或减小；阴影半径不等于临界撞击参数，由此获得弦密度参数的观测上限；弱场偏折角含局部引力项与全局锥形几何项；Shapiro时延含与距离相关的非闵氏渐近贡献；成像显示弦云导致非均匀测地线映射，小阴影未必对应内缩爱因斯坦环，反而可能引起外移。

Conclusion: 广义弦云黑洞的光学信号具有多尺度、非单调、非自相似特征，单一观测量（如阴影大小）不足以唯一确定模型参数；必须联合多种光学探针（阴影、偏折、时延、环结构）才能有效检验该类拓扑缺陷时空。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the optical signatures of black holes surrounded by a generalized cloud of strings, described by the Letelier--Alencar spacetime. We first analyze null geodesics, showing that the string parameters can either increase or decrease the photon sphere radius and the critical impact parameter relative to their Schwarzschild values. Since the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, the critical impact parameter does not directly coincide with the physical shadow radius. We use this distinction to derive constraints on the model parameters from the shadow radius bounds of Sgr A* and M87*. In the weak-field regime, we calculate the light deflection angle using both the Gauss--Bonnet method and a perturbative expansion of the null geodesic equations, obtaining consistent results that contain a contribution from the asymptotically conical geometry in addition to the local gravitational deflection. We also examine the Shapiro time delay and identify a distance dependent contribution associated with the non Minkowskian asymptotic background. Finally, we study the optical appearance of geometrically and optically thin accretion disks and construct celestial sphere images through backward ray tracing. The transfer functions and lensing images show that the generalized cloud of strings modifies the null geodesic mapping in a nonuniform manner rather than producing a simple rescaling of the Schwarzschild image. In particular, configurations with a smaller shadow may still produce an outward displacement of the main Einstein ring image, demonstrating that different optical observables probe distinct aspects of photon propagation in this geometry.

</details>


### [110] [Exact Solutions for Colliding Neutrino Waves and the Absence of Mutual Scattering for a Single Grassmann Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04321)
*Yorgo Senikoglu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-外尔耦合系统中两个对头传播的平面中微子波的碰撞，发现由于Weyl旋量双线性形式的幂零性，两波无散射地穿透彼此；系统在相互作用区可精确求解，且Weyl标量Ψ₂=0，Ψ₀、Ψ₄正比于中微子能流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中微子波在强引力场中的非线性相互作用，特别是广义相对论与量子场论（以Grassmann值Weyl旋量表征）耦合下的散射行为。

Method: 将无质量Weyl旋量视为奇Grassmann场，在Einstein-Weyl系统中应用头对头平面波几何；利用混合分量Einstein方程约束波模式结构；采用O'Brien-Synge非冲量拼接条件匹配单波与相互作用区解；严格解析求解度规与旋量场。

Result: 两反向波必须基于单一Grassmann模，其双线性ε=ηη*幂零；相互作用区内度规函数无波间耦合项；Weyl标量Ψ₂恒为零，Ψ₀与Ψ₄分别正比于左右行进中微子的能量通量；两波完全穿透，无相互散射。

Conclusion: 幂零性是导致中微子波在Einstein-Weyl系统中无散射穿透的根本原因；该结论在纯平面波、无自旋耦合及Grassmann代数封闭假设下成立；交叉耦合可能在引入质量、高阶Grassmann项或弯曲背景涨落时出现。

Abstract: We study the head-on collision of two plane neutrino waves in the coupled Einstein-Weyl system, treating the massless Weyl spinor as an odd Grassmann valued field. We show that the mixed components of the Einstein equations force the two counter propagating waves to be built on a single Grassmann mode, whose bilinear $\varepsilon=ηη^{*}$ is nilpotent. With this structure the full coupled system is solved exactly in the interaction region for arbitrary amplitude and phase profiles, and the metric and spinor fields are matched to the single wave regions by the non-impulsive O'Brien-Synge junction conditions. The metric functions contain no terms coupling the two waves; the Weyl scalar $Ψ_2$ vanishes, and $Ψ_0$, $Ψ_4$ are proportional to the neutrino energy fluxes. The waves therefore traverse each other without mutual scattering; we trace this to the nilpotency of the single mode bilinear. We discuss the limits of this result and indicate where cross couplings can arise.

</details>


### [111] [Bouncing Dust Collapse and Black-to-White Hole Transition on the Brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04353)
*Rikpratik Sengupta,Chiranjeeb Singha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Shtanov-Sahni类时额外维膜世界中三维膜上均匀尘埃云的球对称坍缩，发现在高能区存在由负张力λ控制的密度临界值ρc=2λ；内部解精确可积，存在受λ约束的最小尺度因子a*及非奇点反弹；外部匹配为带正潮汐荷的非静态充电动态Vaidya时空；当质量超过阈值时形成陷获区域但仍发生局部反弹；通过拼接先进与延迟Vaidya解构造C^{1,1}类全局解，尘埃云经白洞区域重出至第二渐近区；广义相对论极限下恢复Oppenheimer-Snyder解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究类时额外维膜世界（Shtanov-Sahni模型）中引力坍缩的非奇点行为，检验高能修正（如ρ²项）对黑洞形成、奇点定理及宇宙审查假说的影响，并对比Randall-Sundrum模型中潮汐荷符号的物理差异。

Method: 采用球对称几何下的膜世界Einstein方程与Israel匹配条件；内部求解封闭空间均匀尘埃的精确Friedmann动力学；外部构造具有迹自由有效源的spherically symmetric外解；通过拼接先进/延迟充电动态Vaidya度规实现全程C^{1,1}匹配；分析曲率不变量有界性、测地完备性及有效能量条件。

Result: 发现内部存在λ决定的最小尺度因子a*=√(3/4πλ)，所有曲率不变量被λ严格上界，测地线完备；外部为正潮汐荷（Q>0）、非静态的充电动态Vaidya时空；陷获区域形成阈值为M>2/(3√(πλ))，但反弹仍在边界邻域发生；全局C^{1,1}拼接解支持白洞型再发射，尘埃云进入第二渐近时空；GR极限λ→∞下严格退化为Oppenheimer-Snyder解。

Conclusion: 在Shtanov-Sahni类时膜世界中，均匀尘埃球的引力坍缩不产生时空奇点，而是经历有界曲率的周期性反弹，并通过白洞机制实现拓扑非平凡的时空演化；该模型为规避经典奇点定理提供了自洽的高能引力框架，且潮汐荷符号反转凸显了类时额外维的几何本质区别。

Abstract: We study the spherical collapse of a homogeneous dust cloud on a three-brane in the Shtanov--Sahni braneworld, in which the extra dimension is timelike. In the high-energy regime the brane Friedmann equation acquires a negative quadratic term in the matter density, controlled by a critical density $\rhoc=2λ$, where $λ$ is the magnitude of the negative brane tension. For a spatially closed interior we integrate the dynamics exactly. Every turning point of the scale factor lies above $\astar=\sqrt{3/4πλ}$, a bounce exists if and only if a simple condition on the comoving density holds, and all curvature invariants stay below bounds fixed by $λ$ alone along the entire cycle. Comoving worldlines are geodesically complete. We then match the cloud across a comoving boundary to a spherically symmetric exterior with a trace-free effective source and solve the Israel conditions in closed form. The exterior is a charged Vaidya geometry whose mass function and tidal charge are fixed by the boundary radius. The tidal charge is positive, opposite in sign to the Randall--Sundrum case, and the exterior cannot be static. A trapped region forms on the boundary if and only if the mass exceeds $2/(3\sqrt{πλ})$, and in that case the bounce still takes place in an untrapped neighbourhood of the boundary. For trapped clouds we construct an exterior over the full contraction, bounce and re-expansion by joining an advanced and a retarded charged Vaidya patch through a static wedge. The matched metric is of class $C^{1,1}$, the effective energy conditions hold, and the cloud re-emerges through a white hole region into a second asymptotic region. In the general relativistic limit $λ\to\infty$ the Oppenheimer--Snyder solution is recovered.

</details>


### [112] [On the stability of spherically symmetric relativistic configurations with scalar field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04360)
*Anatolii Tugay,Valery Zhdanov,Yurii Taistra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究广义相对论中带幂律势的最小耦合标量场球对称静态渐近平直构型对径向（单极）扰动的线性稳定性，采用新射击法求解发散形式的二阶‘主方程’，发现小标量荷构型存在指数增长的不稳定模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于此类标量场构型通常导致裸奇点，需探究其动力学稳定性以评估其物理可实现性。

Method: 发展一种新型射击法，将径向扰动稳定性问题约化为一个具正则化有效势的二阶发散型‘主方程’，并从中提取对应指数增长模的本征值。

Result: 确认存在对应于指数增长扰动模的负本征值，且该不稳定模仅出现在初始静态构型标量荷足够小时。

Conclusion: 这类含幂律势标量场的球对称静态解在小标量荷参数下是线性不稳定的，支持其作为裸奇点候选解缺乏动力学鲁棒性。

Abstract: Static spherically symmetric asymptotically flat configurations in General Relativity with a minimally coupled scalar field typically possess naked singularities. We study a linear stability of these configurations against radial (monopole) perturbations in case of power-law scalar field potentials. We employ a new shooting method that extracts the eigenvalues corresponding to the exponentially growing modes of the perturbation. The problem is reduced to one second order ``master'' equation in a divergent form with a regularized effective potential. We confirm our previous results showing that instability modes exist for sufficiently small scalar charges of the initial static configurations.

</details>


### [113] [Bias-aware Fisher forecasts for stochastic gravitational-wave background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04459)
*Bo-Qiang Lu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机引力波背景（SGWB）Whittle似然预测中复合两阶段估计器的误差膨胀问题，发现其在11.5天基线下可使振幅误差棒增大达一个数量级；指出该膨胀源于估计器结构而非gauss_D似然近似本身；通过与精确Whittle似然对比，校准了幂律与分段幂律模板下的偏差向量和均方误差矩阵，并揭示了多参数情形下传统单参数Whittle偏差诊断的失效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whittle似然方法是mHz频段空间干涉仪（如LISA、Taiji、TianQin）探测随机引力波背景的核心统计工具，但实际应用中广泛采用的复合两阶段估计器存在未被充分认识的系统性误差膨胀，亟需定量刻画与校准。

Method: 以精确Whittle似然为控制基准，gauss_D为工作模型，对幂律（PL）与光滑折断幂律（SBPL）模板进行偏差向量与均方误差矩阵（MSE matrix）的频次主义校准；结合32点声壳扫描尖锐信号实验、不同时间分割（segmentation）长度分析及信噪比相关性检验，系统评估误差膨胀行为R≡σ_composite/σ_exact及其依赖关系。

Result: 1) 复合估计器在11.5天基线下导致振幅误差棒膨胀因子R=10.2（中位数≈3），且R²−1∝T_seg²；存在不可消除的效率损失Cb，最高达α_out参数的18%；2) 单参数Whittle偏差诊断无法反映多参数误差膨胀，方向性错误；3) 在30分钟分段下膨胀可忽略（R−1≤1.5×10⁻³），且R−1与ln(SNR)强相关（corr=0.91）；4) 校准结果仅适用于强信号、平稳过程及匹配噪声模型。

Conclusion: 复合两阶段估计器引入显著且非平凡的误差膨胀，不应作为默认选择；推荐采用联合最大化的gauss_D似然（其本身无偏，R=1.000±10⁻⁴）作为基准；所获偏差与MSE校准结果为高精度SGWB参数估计提供了关键频次主义修正依据，但其贝叶斯可信区间外推仅在局部高斯近似下成立。

Abstract: Whittle-likelihood forecasting of the stochastic gravitational-wave background underpins planned mHz interferometers (LISA, Taiji, TianQin). A composite two-stage estimator, per-bin amplitude estimation then unweighted log-space template fitting, inflates the marginal-Fisher amplitude error bar on resolved-bin count by up to an order of magnitude at \(11.5\)-d baseline. This inflation arises from the composite implementation, not the determinant-approximate \texttt{gauss\_D} likelihood it approximates. Jointly maximised, \texttt{gauss\_D} is unbiased (\(R=1.000\) within \(10^{-4}\)) and is recommended baseline with exact-Whittle likelihood. With exact Whittle as control and \texttt{gauss\_D} as working model, we calibrate bias vector and mean-squared-error matrix for power-law and smooth broken-power-law templates. First, for sharp signals of a \(32\)-point sound-shell scan, the composite estimator inflates amplitude error bar by \(R=10.2\) at \(11.5\)-d baseline (median \(\simeq3\)), follows \(R^2-1\propto T_{\mathrm{seg}}^2\) at shorter segments, and has a non-removable efficiency gap \(\mathbf{C}_b\) up to \(18\%\) in \(α_{\mathrm{out}}\). Second, the usual single-parameter Whittle-bias diagnostic does not track multi-parameter inflation and errs both ways. Third, inflation is negligible on \(30\)-min segments, \(R-1\le1.5\times10^{-3}\) over measured bias-exponent range, vanishes below sensitivity floor, and tracks resolved signal strength with \(\mathrm{corr}[\ln \mathrm{SNR},\ln(R-1)]=0.91\). Results are frequentist; interval diagnostics transfer to flat-prior credible intervals only in the locally Gaussian regime. The calibration is a strong-signal, stationary statement transferable only to a matching noise model.

</details>


### [114] [Magnetic-field-induced chaos of charged particles around a rotating black hole in a King-type dark matter halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04521)
*Yu Wang,Meilin Liu,Haiguang Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在King型暗物质晕和外部均匀磁场共同作用下，绕旋转黑洞运动的带电测试粒子的动力学行为；通过Poincaré截面与Lyapunov指数等混沌诊断工具，发现磁耦合增强可诱发由规则运动向混沌的转变，且暗物质晕密度显著影响混沌程度，但部分弱晕效应在数值精度下不可分辨；同时存在即使有非零磁耦合仍保持规则运动的初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质晕与外部磁场对黑洞周围带电粒子轨道稳定性的影响，特别是探究广义相对论框架下非真空、非可积背景中混沌动力学的起源与调制机制。

Method: 采用修正Newman–Janis方法构造含King型暗物质晕的旋转有效度规；引入需支撑电流的测试电磁场；利用哈密顿–雅可比方程分离性建立无磁耦合时的可积参考系统；结合Poincaré截面、快速Lyapunov指标（FLI）和有限时间Lyapunov指数（FTLE）进行轨道稳定性分析，并辅以质量壳约束与数值收敛性检验。

Result: 磁耦合增强可导致规则轨道向混沌转变，表现为Poincaré截面弥散及持续正的FTLE；高密度King晕显著增大典型混沌轨道的FTLE，而弱晕修正在数值精度假设下未被一致解析；存在特定初始条件下，即使存在磁耦合，轨道仍呈现闭合Poincaré曲线与衰减的FTLE，表明规则运动依然存在。

Conclusion: King型暗物质晕以初始条件依赖的方式调制磁场诱导的轨道不稳定性；磁化系统中规则运动并未被完全抑制，混沌与规则共存，凸显了暗物质分布与电磁相互作用在强引力场动力学中的协同调控作用。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged test particles around a rotating black hole embedded in a King-type dark matter halo and subjected to a prescribed, asymptotically uniform magnetic field. We adopt an effective rotating geometry constructed from a static halo seed through a modified Newman--Janis prescription and a test electromagnetic field that generally requires supporting currents in the nonvacuum background. In the absence of magnetic coupling, the timelike Hamilton--Jacobi equation separates and admits a Carter-type constant, providing an integrable reference system. We characterize the orbital dynamics using Poincaré sections and tangent-vector growth diagnostics, including fast Lyapunov indicators and finite-time Lyapunov exponents, with mass-shell monitoring and numerical refinement checks. For the sampled initial conditions, increasing the magnetic coupling can produce a transition from regular motion to scattered Poincaré sections with sustained positive finite-time growth rates. At fixed magnetic coupling, the two higher-density halo cases yield larger finite-time exponents for a representative chaotic orbit family, whereas the weak-halo correction is not resolved consistently under numerical refinement. A different initial condition yields a closed Poincaré curve and a decreasing finite-time exponent over an extended integration, supporting regular motion despite the nonzero magnetic coupling. These results show that the King-type halo modulates magnetic-field-induced orbital instability in an initial-condition-dependent manner, while regular motion persists within the magnetized system.

</details>


### [115] [Exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations in null homogeneous Petrov spaces with solvable motions groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04522)
*V. V. Obukhov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了零齐次Petrov空间 $V^*_4(N)$ 的几何结构及其上不变电磁场的真空Maxwell方程解，利用Bianchi分类中可解三维运动群 $G_3(I)-G_3(VII)$ 对应的齐次Riemann空间 $V_3(N)$ 的标架，构造了四维度规与矢量势，并显式求得所有非等价真空Maxwell解，其形式为波变量的任意函数；该结果将Einstein-Maxwell方程组的求解简化为仅含这些函数的常微分方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立具有齐次结构的Petrov型N类时空上不变电磁场的严格解析解框架，为求解Einstein-Maxwell系统提供几何简化路径。

Method: 基于Bianchi分类中可解三参数运动群 $G_3(N)$ 作用下的三维齐次Riemann空间 $V_3(N)$，采用其正则非完整标架构造四维零齐次Petrov空间 $V^*_4(N)$ 的度规与电磁矢量势；在该标架下将Maxwell真空方程约化为仅含群结构常数、非全纯度规分量、矢量势分量及其对波变量导数的方程；系统求解所有非等价解。

Result: 对 $G_3(I)$ 至 $G_3(VII)$ 所有可解Bianchi类，完全分类并显式给出所有非等价真空Maxwell场解，解以波变量 $u$ 的任意光滑函数形式表达；由此，Einstein-Maxwell方程中Maxwell部分被完全消去，剩余Einstein方程退化为关于这些函数的常微分方程组。

Conclusion: 零齐次Petrov空间上的不变电磁场真空解具有由Bianchi群结构决定的普适函数自由度；该方法实现了Maxwell约束的完全解析消去，为后续求解耦合引力-电磁场方程提供了可积性基础。

Abstract: Null homogeneous Petrov spaces $V^*_4(N)$ are considered. The geometry of $V^*_4(N)$ is determined by the geometry of the three-dimensional homogeneous Riemannian space $V_3(N)$, which is invariant under the action of the three-parameter motions group $G_3(N)$ ($N$ corresponds to the group number in the Bianchi classification). The canonical frame of the space $V_3(N)$ is used to construct the metric tensor of the space $V^*_4(N)$ and the components of the vector potential of the invariant electromagnetic field. Using this reper, we obtain Maxwell vacuum equations containing only the structure constants of the groups, the non-holonomic components of the contravariant metric tensor and vector potential, and their derivatives with respect to the wave variable. All non-equivalent exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations are found for the case of solvable motions groups $G_3(I)-G_3(VII)$.
  All solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations obtained in this article are written explicitly in terms of arbitrary independent functions of wave variable. Therefore, when integrating the Einstein-Maxwell equations, it has to consider only the remaining systems of Einstein equations which reduce to systems of ordinary differential equations that include only these independent functions.

</details>


### [116] [Dynamic Quantum Wormholes: Path-Integral State Selection and the Quantum Throat Trajectory in an Exactly Solvable Thin-Shell Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04577)
*Rikpratik Sengupta,Paulo Vargas Moniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个球对称薄壳虫洞的minisuperspace量子化模型，基于导波（pilot-wave）量子宇宙学框架；对无压壳层，Wheeler–DeWitt方程严格化为Whittaker方程（M→0时退化为修正Bessel方程）；在渐近平直外时空下，波函数在a→∞处的有界性唯一确定一个实解，其Bohm流恒为零，故喉部冻结；该态是正则化Lorentzian路径积分在ε→0⁺下的精确极限，并通过resolvent解析性和Picard–Lefschetz柄计算验证（精度达48位）；Whittaker连接公式闭式解决近喉自伴延拓歧义；若外时空换为Schwarzschild–de Sitter，则势能在大a处趋于−∞，出现真正动力学的纯出射态，具有非零守恒Bohm流，描述一长寿命亚稳态虫洞，以与WKB一致的速率隧穿至无界膨胀；Airy连接公式给出亚临界耦合下剩余预因子κ_∞=0.814±0.004，其解析起源未追溯，但物理结果不依赖它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动态球对称薄壳虫洞的量子行为，尤其在导波诠释下是否存在非平凡动力学演化，以及边界条件、自伴延拓和路径积分正则化之间的内在一致性。

Method: Minisuperspace约化（仅保留喉半径a(τ)为自由度），求解Israel拼接条件导出的Wheeler–DeWitt方程；应用Whittaker与Airy特殊函数理论处理方程的渐近与匹配；利用resolvent解析性及Picard–Lefschetz thimble方法分析Lorentzian路径积分极限；通过Bohmian力学计算量子流并判定动力学活性；采用自伴算子理论（Whittaker连接公式）解决近喉边界条件歧义。

Result: 在渐近平直外时空下，唯一物理态为实波函数，Bohm流恒零，喉部完全冻结；该态精确对应ε→0⁺极限下的正则化Lorentzian路径积分；在SdS外时空下，存在纯出射、非零Bohm流的动力学态，支持长寿命亚稳态虫洞及其隧穿至无限膨胀；κ_∞数值由Airy连接确定但解析起源未明。

Conclusion: 虫洞的量子动力学强烈依赖于外时空渐近结构：平直时空导致量子冻结，而正宇宙常数引入负无穷远势阱，恢复真实量子演化；导波诠释下，Bohm流是否非零是判别量子动力学活性的本质标准；路径积分、WdW方程与自伴延拓三者在严格数学框架下自洽统一。

Abstract: We construct a minisuperspace quantization of a dynamic, spherically symmetric thin-shell wormhole in the spirit of pilot-wave quantum cosmology, treating the throat radius $a(τ)$ as the single degree of freedom of the Israel junction-condition constraint. For a pressureless shell of mass $M$ the Wheeler--DeWitt equation reduces exactly to Whittaker's equation, and to the modified Bessel equation as $M\to0$. When the exterior is asymptotically flat, boundedness of the wave function as $a\to\infty$ selects a unique state that can always be chosen real. Since any real solution has a vanishing Bohmian current, the throat is frozen for any linear equation of state and any operator ordering, independently of the choice between Euclidean and Lorentzian boundary conditions. This state is the exact $ε\to0^+$ limit of the regularized Lorentzian path integral, as shown by resolvent analyticity and by an exact Picard--Lefschetz thimble calculation that agrees with the closed-form solution to $48$ digits. The near-throat self-adjointness ambiguity is resolved in closed form through the Whittaker connection formula. Replacing the exterior by Schwarzschild--de~Sitter removes the obstruction: the potential tends to $-\infty$ at large $a$, and the equation admits a genuinely dynamic, purely outgoing state. Propagated exactly through the classically forbidden barrier, it carries a conserved, nonzero Bohmian current everywhere, including the inner pocket. It describes a long-lived metastable wormhole that tunnels into unbounded expansion at a rate consistent with the WKB estimate. Airy connection formulas leave a residual prefactor constant $κ_\infty=0.814\pm0.004$ for sub-critical coupling, which we do not trace to its analytic origin, and the physical results do not depend on it.

</details>


### [117] [Quasinormal-Mode Collider Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04595)
*Mian Zhu,Jun Tao,Peng Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出准正规模碰撞谱学（QCS）框架，用于系统研究黑洞非线性环-down过程中不同准正规模之间的相互作用；证明了立方应变残差在孤立非简并中间极点处可因子化，并可通过视界与无穷远辐射重构中间极点残差，从而建立低阶辐射与高阶可观测量之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞谱学将准正规模视为独立线性振荡，无法揭示其非线性相互作用及中介态；需新框架刻画非线性ringdown的模式耦合结构。

Method: 借鉴宇宙学对撞机物理思想，构建准正规模碰撞谱学（QCS）；运用复频域格林函数分析、极点留数分解与边界辐射匹配技术，推导立方应变残差的因子化性质及中间极点残差的全息重构定理。

Result: 1) 在孤立非简并中间极点处，立方应变残差可分解为激发振幅与二次转换系数之积，导致输出通道间秩约束；2) 中间极点残差可由视界与无穷远辐射唯一确定，且该重构可推广至固定一阶场与三阶数据下、改变二阶初值所生成的完整立方波形差。

Conclusion: QCS为探测黑洞非线性ringdown的相互作用结构提供了严格理论框架，实现了从线性谱学到非线性谱学的范式拓展，并为强场引力检验引入新的可观测量与自洽性检验方法。

Abstract: Black-hole (BH) quasinormal modes encode the properties of merger remnants and provide powerful tests of strong-field gravity. The BH spectroscopy has predominantly treated them as independent linear oscillations. Nonlinear ringdown can additionally reveal how these modes interact and which intermediate states mediate their interactions. Inspired by cosmological collider physics, we introduce quasinormal-mode collider spectroscopy (QCS), a framework for organizing and predicting nonlinear BH responses. We establish two structural results. First, at an isolated, nondegenerate intermediate pole, a cubic strain residue factorizes into an excitation amplitude and a quadratic conversion coefficient. This factorization imposes rank constraints across output channels and choices of second-order initial data, providing consistency tests of the intermediate-mode description. Second, the intermediate pole residue can be reconstructed from its horizon and infinity radiation. With the first-order field and third-order data held fixed, this reconstruction extends to the full cubic waveform difference generated by changing the second-order initial data. QCS thus connects lower-order radiation to higher-order observables and provides a framework for probing the interaction structure of nonlinear ringdown.

</details>


### [118] [Covariance scaling and a dual-mode torque diagnostic in measurements of the gravitational constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04707)
*Vineet Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对CODATA-2018中16个G值测量结果进行协方差尺度建模分析，发现历史数据存在显著超出报道不确定度的离散性（λ̂=2.967）；NIST 2026年四组态扭秤结果在引入历史标度因子后与档案一致（p=0.382），且其自由-伺服扭矩差支持统一绝对偏移模型（ΔN=1.918±0.215 pN·m），而非比例缩放模型（Δχ²=11.041）。文章未给出G的新推荐值，而提供协方差审计、谱系加权一致性检验及双模扭秤可验证诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决G值实验测量间长期存在的不可调和离散性问题，检验CODATA-2018档案中各测量是否满足共同协方差结构假设，并厘清NIST新测量与历史数据（尤其BIPM Mark II谱系）的统计相容性。

Method: 采用比例协方差尺度模型（proportional covariance-scale model），保留全部报道协方差矩阵；对15个非BIPM-14测量进行历史拟合得尺度因子λ̂；对NIST四配置结果实施Q_λ检验与有限样本F检验；对NIST自由-伺服扭矩差构建绝对偏移与比例缩放两类嵌套模型，进行χ²对比。

Result: 历史尺度因子λ̂=2.967；NIST结果在λ标度下Q_λ=4.840，p=0.382，统计相容；四组扭矩差支持统一绝对偏移ΔN=1.918±0.215 pN·m，拒绝等效比例模型（Δχ²=11.041，显著性>99.9%）。

Conclusion: G值测量间的系统离散主要源于未报道的共性系统误差（如扭丝非线性、静电残余、几何标定偏差），而非随机误差低估；NIST新数据与历史档案在适当协方差再标度下自洽；提出的绝对扭矩偏移诊断可作为双模扭秤系统误差的实验判据。

Abstract: Laboratory determinations of the Newtonian gravitational constant $G$ disagree by more than their quoted uncertainties allow. We examine the sixteen determinations comprising the CODATA-2018 archive using a proportional covariance-scale model that retains the full reported covariance matrix. Because the 2026 NIST torsion-balance redetermination shares apparatus lineage with the BIPM Mark II balance, our primary historical fit excludes the BIPM-14 determination, giving $\hatλ = 2.967$ for the remaining fifteen measurements. Evaluated against this historical model under the declared uniform covariance-scaling convention, the correlated four-configuration NIST result is highly unlikely under the archive's reported, unscaled covariance but is statistically unremarkable once the historically inferred extra dispersion is applied ($Q_λ= 4.840$; finite-sample $F$ tail $p = 0.382$); a mean-only scaling sensitivity leaves the vector discrepant. Retaining the complete archive also gives a comparable result. We further examine four matched free-minus-servo torque differences reported by NIST. They are consistent with a single common absolute torque offset across the four tested configurations, $\widehat{ΔN} = 1.918 \pm 0.215$ pN m, and poorly described by an equally simple fractional-scale alternative under the stated independent-Type-A treatment, with a chi-square contrast of $Δχ^{2} = 11.041$ between the two torque models. The paper provides a covariance audit of the historical archive, a lineage-qualified consistency check, and an experimentally testable diagnostic for dual-mode torsion balances, without proposing a revised value of $G$.

</details>


### [119] [Polyakov backreaction in large D discretely self-similar collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04960)
*Sheng-Hong Lai,Wen-Yu Wen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登理论中离散自相似解在反常诱导的半经典背反应下的行为，发现最小耦合Polyakov模型无法给出经典中心的一般光滑、精确自相似延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子反常如何影响强引力场中自相似结构的半经典稳定性与正则性，尤其关注黑洞形成临界现象中的中心奇点结构。

Method: 采用球对称约化下的高维Einstein–Klein–Gordon系统，结合Polyakov反常能动张量构造半经典方程；利用Floquet理论分析null坐标下辅助场的仿周期性；通过匹配内外区（零质量外区与非线性内区）求解奇异面与临界喉部结构。

Result: 发现Polyakov辅助场具有由共形标度与全局零模条件共同决定的单值性（monodromy）；存在一个临界胀子曲面，其一般穿越导致奇异性；仅当匹配到正则零质量外解时，才出现窄而高曲率的喉部；反常使有效零能条件曲线与经典物质流及零曲率曲线分离。

Conclusion: 最小耦合Polyakov模型不能实现经典中心的一般光滑、精确自相似完成；量子反常从根本上破坏了该类解的正则延拓可能性，暗示需更完备的量子引力效应或非最小耦合机制。

Abstract: We study anomaly-induced semiclassical backreaction on an analytic family of discretely self-similar solutions of Einstein--Klein--Gordon theory at large spacetime dimension. Horizon regularity fixes a null-coordinate Floquet exponent and selects a unique regular quantum state. The Polyakov auxiliary field is affine-periodic rather than periodic, with a monodromy that is determined independently by a global zero-mode condition and by the conformal-coordinate scaling. Spherical reduction introduces a quantum coupling that grows toward the center, so the periodic first-order expansion is nonuniform. We construct the resulting nonlinear inner problem and find a critical dilaton surface. Generic crossings of this surface are singular, whereas matching to the regular zero-mass exterior selects a narrow, high-curvature throat. The anomaly also separates the effective null-energy boundaries from the classical matter flux and zero-curvature curves. These results show that the minimally coupled Polyakov model does not yield a generic smooth, exactly self-similar completion of the classical center.

</details>


### [120] [Thermodynamic Volumes of Extended Black Hole Thermodynamics with Auxiliary Gauge Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05022)
*Zili Mou,Yu Tian,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用Iyer-Wald形式论，通过对含动力学标量场（由原拉格朗日量中的耦合常数如宇宙学常数提升而来）与辅助规范场的修正引力作用量进行变分，导出了扩展黑洞热力学第一定律；所得共轭于耦合常数的热力学体积不依赖于辅助规范场，且与直接对耦合常数变分所得结果一致；进一步证明该体积亦可表为辅助规范场在黑洞视界上的积分，从而验证了先前文献中的提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理耦合常数（如宇宙学常数）的动力学化及其在黑洞热力学中的角色，解决热力学体积定义的协变性与几何诠释问题。

Method: 应用Iyer-Wald形式论于含辅助规范场的推广引力拉格朗日量，将耦合常数提升为动力学标量场；通过诺特定理与边界项分析推导广义热力学第一定律；计算共轭热力学体积并比对其在不同表述下的等价性。

Result: 1) 得到协变、不依赖辅助规范场的热力学体积表达式，与‘暴力变分’法结果完全一致；2) 证明该体积可等价地写为辅助规范场在事件视界上的通量积分，确立其几何意义。

Conclusion: Iyer-Wald框架下耦合常数的动力学化方案自洽且几何清晰；热力学体积具有双重表象：既为耦合常数的共轭变量，又为辅助规范场在视界上的守恒荷，从而为扩展黑洞热力学提供了坚实的形式基础与物理解释。

Abstract: By applying the Iyer-Wald formalism to the modified Lagrangian form for the gravitational system, where the coupling constants appearing in the original Lagrangian form, such as the cosmological constant, are promoted to the dynamical scalar fields by introducing the corresponding auxiliary gauge fields, we derive the first law of the extended black hole thermodynamics, where the resulting thermodynamic volumes as the conjugate thermodynamic variables to the coupling constants are nevertheless independent of the auxiliary gauge fields and exactly the same as those obtained before via varying the coupling constants by brute force. In addition, it is further shown that such volumes can also be expressed in terms of the auxiliary gauge fields and evaluated on the black hole horizon, validating the proposal made in the previous literature.

</details>


### [121] [Thermodynamic Stability of a Viscous Variable Generalised Chaplygin Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05361)
*D. Panigrahi,K. Kanjilal,Saibal Ray*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平坦FLRW宇宙中，粘性可变广义Chaplygin气体（VVGCG）模型的宇宙学动力学与热力学性质；该模型的状态方程为 P = -B/ρ^α，其中 B = B₀V^{n/3}，并引入体积依赖的体粘滞压强 Π = -3ξH，ξ = ξ₀ρ^{1/2}；结果表明，当 ξ₀ < 0 且 n < 0 时，模型同时满足热力学稳定性（c_V > 0）与宇宙演化要求：早期减速、晚期幻影型加速，并符合第三定律（T→0 时 S→0），且与 Hubble-57 观测数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个既能统一暗物质与暗能量、又满足热力学基本定律（尤其是第三定律与稳定性条件）且能再现观测宇宙演化历史（早期减速→晚期加速）的物态模型；克服传统Chaplygin气体模型在热力学一致性或动力学行为上的局限。

Method: 在空间平坦FLRW度规下，将VVGCG作为唯象流体建模，结合Einstein场方程与能量守恒方程，求解宇宙标度因子演化；引入体粘滞压强Π = -3ξH（ξ ∝ ρ^{1/2}）以刻画非平衡效应；系统推导热力学量（熵S、热容c_V、等温/绝热声速、稳定性判据）并验证热力学恒等式dU = TdS − PdV + μdN；通过Hubble-57哈勃参数数据对参数α, n, ξ₀作χ²拟合约束。

Result: 发现物理可行分支为ξ₀ < 0：早期正粘滞压强主导，导致减速膨胀；存在临界体积V_c使有效压强变号，V_f > V_c处发生加速转变；大体积极限下状态方程参数w → w_∞ < −1（幻影区）当n < 0；热力学分析表明仅当n < 0且ξ₀ < 0时，c_V > 0与（等温/绝热）稳定性条件同时成立；熵S → 0（T → 0），存在有限最小体积V_min与对应最大温度T_max；Hubble-57拟合得α ≈ 0.0024, n ≈ −0.0182, ξ₀ ≈ −0.0015，落于稳定参数区。

Conclusion: VVGCG模型在满足完整热力学一致性（含第三定律、正热容、双稳定性）前提下，自洽描述从辐射/物质主导减速期到幻影型晚期加速的全宇宙演化历程；其参数受观测严格限制，且退化为多个已有Chaplygin类模型（如GCG、VCG等），具备理论普适性与观测可检验性。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics and thermodynamic properties of a viscous variable generalized Chaplygin gas (VVGCG) model in a spatially flat Friedmann--Lema^ıtre--Robertson--Walker universe, described by $P=-B/ρ^α$, where $B=B_0V^{n/3}$. The model includes a bulk-viscous pressure $Π=-3ξH$, with $ξ=ξ_0ρ^{1/2}$. We find that the physically viable branch corresponds to $ξ_0<0$, for which the positive viscous pressure dominates at early times, producing decelerated expansion. As the volume increases, the effective pressure changes sign at a finite volume $V_c$, while the transition to acceleration occurs at a larger volume $V_f$. At large volume, the equation-of-state parameter approaches a phantom-like regime for $n<0$. A detailed thermodynamic analysis of the thermal and caloric equations of state, entropy, heat capacity, and isothermal and adiabatic stability shows that the stability conditions and $c_V>0$ are simultaneously satisfied for $n<0$ and $ξ_0<0$. The entropy vanishes as $T\rightarrow0$, consistent with the third law, while the model admits a finite minimum volume and corresponding maximum temperature. Thermodynamic consistency is further verified through the fundamental thermodynamic identity. The framework also contains several existing Chaplygin-gas models as special cases. Constraints from the Hubble-57 data yield $α=0.0024$, $n=-0.0182$, and $ξ_0=-0.0015$, consistent with the thermodynamically stable parameter region. Thus, the VVGCG model provides a consistent description of cosmic evolution from an early decelerating phase to late-time phantom-like acceleration while remaining thermodynamically stable throughout the evolution.

</details>


### [122] [Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes with NUT charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05656)
*Masato Nozawa,Tomohiro Harada,Keisuke Nakashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了Einstein-phantom-scalar理论中带NUT荷的Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞的精确解，该解无曲率奇点、渐近局域平坦（或AdS），具有Taub-NUT几何特征（如Dirac-Misner弦和闭合类时曲线），但无Killing视界，从而保证测地完备性；进一步引入含无穷多AdS真空的标量势，得到连接不同真空的渐近AdS虫洞；利用协变相空间方法证明其哈密顿荷有限，并通过双Wick转动揭示其共形类具有ambi-Kähler结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造兼具NUT荷与虫洞拓扑的正则、完备、物理可实现的时空解，弥合理论上NUT几何与虫洞物理（如可穿越性、能量条件违背）之间的张力，并探索其在全息与几何结构（如ambi-Kähler）中的深层含义。

Method: 基于Einstein-phantom-scalar作用量，求解球对称稳态场方程；采用协变相空间形式主义计算渐近荷；施加适配的标量场边界条件以确保荷的有限性；执行双Wick转动至Euclidean号差并分析共形几何结构。

Result: 获得两个精确解：(i) 渐近局域平坦、测地完备、无奇点、具NUT荷的phantom wormhole；(ii) 渐近局域AdS、连接不同AdS真空的NUT wormhole；证明其ADM/NUT荷有限；Euclidean化后度规共形类承载ambi-Kähler结构。

Conclusion: NUT荷可与phantom驱动的虫洞自洽共存，且不破坏测地完备性；此类解为研究NUT物理、AdS/CFT中多真空隧穿及四维Kähler几何的引力实现提供了新模型。

Abstract: We construct an exact NUT-charged generalization of the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole in the Einstein-phantom-scalar system. The solution is stationary, asymptotically locally flat at both ends and free of curvature singularities. It inherits characteristic features of the Taub-NUT geometry, including the Dirac-Misner string and closed timelike curves, while the absence of Killing horizons eliminates the horizon-induced infinite winding of geodesics and renders the spacetime geodesically complete. By allowing the phantom field to have a nontrivial scalar potential with an infinite sequence of AdS vacua, we also obtain an exact asymptotically locally AdS wormhole with a NUT charge, which interpolates between different vacua at its two asymptotic ends. Using the covariant phase space formalism, we show that the Hamiltonian charges associated with the two asymptotic regions are finite under appropriate scalar boundary conditions. By performing a double Wick rotation to Euclidean signature, we demonstrate that the conformal class of the resulting metric admits an ambi-Kähler structure.

</details>


### [123] [Exact black hole solutions in Palatini Kalb-Ramond gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05799)
*Lin-Yi She,Rui-Jie Niu,Ke Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini框架下Kalb-Ramond引力理论中的静态球对称黑洞解，该理论中Kalb-Ramond场的非零真空期望值自发破缺局域洛伦兹对称性；导出了独立度规与无挠仿射联络下的修正场方程，得到了含正、零、负宇宙常数Λ的精确解族；证明Palatini形式允许两个洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ₁和ℓ₂同时非零；利用Iyer-Wald方法计算黑洞守恒质量，并在Λ≤0情形下验证第一定律与Smarr关系，确认该质量即热力学焓；通过水星进动、星光偏折与Shapiro延迟对Λ=0解进行太阳系检验，发现所有观测仅约束组合参数q≡ℓ₂/(1−ℓ₁)，其中水星进动给出最强限制：−7.4×10⁻¹² ≤ q ≤ 3.7×10⁻¹¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹对称性自发破缺（通过Kalb-Ramond场vev）在Palatini变分框架下的引力效应，特别是其对黑洞结构、热力学及太阳系尺度可观测性的系统影响，以区分于纯度规表述并检验低能Lorentz违反现象。

Method: 采用Palatini变分法（度规g_μν与无挠联络Γ^α_βγ独立变分），构造含Kalb-Ramond场B_μν及其势能项的扩展引力作用量；求解静态球对称ansatz下的场方程，获得解析黑洞解；应用Iyer-Wald泛函微分几何方法推导守恒荷（质量）；对Λ=0解实施后牛顿近似，匹配标准PPN参数并拟合水星近日点进动、光线偏折与Shapiro延迟观测数据。

Result: 得到含任意Λ的精确黑洞解族；证实Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力允许ℓ₁, ℓ₂ ≠ 0（而纯度规版本强制ℓ₂ = 0）；守恒质量M被证为Λ ≤ 0黑洞的热力学焓；太阳系检验表明所有三类观测仅敏感于无量纲组合q ≡ ℓ₂/(1−ℓ₁)；水星进动给出q的最紧致约束：−7.4×10⁻¹² ≤ q ≤ 3.7×10⁻¹¹。

Conclusion: Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力提供了一个自洽且可检验的洛伦兹破缺引力模型，其黑洞解具有丰富结构与明确热力学诠释；太阳系精度实验已将有效Lorentz破缺强度约束至亚皮米级（q ∼ 10⁻¹¹），凸显该理论在量子引力低能唯象学中的可观测性。

Abstract: We investigate static and spherically symmetric black holes in Palatini Kalb-Ramond gravity, where a nonzero vacuum expectation value of the Kalb-Ramond field spontaneously breaks local Lorentz symmetry. Treating the metric and the torsion-free affine connection as independent variables, we derive the modified field equations and obtain an exact family of solutions encompassing positive, vanishing, and negative values of the cosmological constant $Λ$. Unlike the corresponding purely metric formulation, the Palatini theory consistently allows both Lorentz-violating parameters $\ell_1$ and $\ell_2$ to be nonzero. Using the Iyer-Wald formalism, we determine the conserved mass of the black hole solutions. For the $Λ\leq0$ sector, we further verify the first law and the Smarr relation, identifying the conserved mass as the thermodynamic enthalpy. We then confront the $Λ=0$ solution with Mercury's perihelion precession, solar light deflection, and the Shapiro time delay. Once the central mass parameter is calibrated by planetary dynamics, these observables constrain the Lorentz-violating parameters only through $q\equiv\ell_2/(1-\ell_1)$. Among these tests, Mercury's perihelion precession yields the most stringent constraint, $-7.4\times10^{-12} \leq q \leq 3.7\times10^{-11}$.

</details>

Method: 采用经典广义相对论框架下的各向异性流体模型，分析奇宇称引力波在该半经典、无视界致密天体表面的边界行为与传播动力学，重点计算其反射率与囚禁时间尺度。

Result: 奇宇称引力波在表面不发生有效反射，而是因极端红移被长期囚禁于物体内，回声延迟时间远大于宇宙年龄（~10^10 年），故回声信号在观测上完全不可分辨。

Conclusion: 该量子黑洞模型自然抑制引力波回声，与LIGO/Virgo等实验的零结果自洽；要区分它与经典黑洞，需依赖其他可观测效应（如吸积盘谱、环down信号或高阶模式激发）。

Abstract: We investigate gravitational-wave echoes in a horizonless compact object model, a non-perturbative solution of the semi-classical Einstein equation that reproduces the entropy-area law from the bulk structure. By employing a model of anisotropic fluids in classical general relativity (GR), we show that odd-parity gravitational waves are not reflected at the object's surface but are trapped inside due to the exponentially strong redshift, and that the echo-delay time extremely exceeds the age of the Universe. Consequently, the echo signals are effectively unobservable, which is consistent with current null results from echo searches. We finally discuss how this quantum black hole and the conventional classical black hole could be distinguished observationally.

</details>


### [125] [Exact Thermodynamics and Two-Horizon Relations of Four Rotating Einstein-Maxwell-Maxwell-Dilaton Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06037)
*Hai Huang,Juhua Chen*

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维渐近平直Einstein-Maxwell-Maxwell-dilaton理论中Kerr-Newman与Kaluza-Klein种子的四类精确旋转嵌入的完备外视界热力学，验证了第一定律、Smarr关系与标量平移恒等式，并发现两正面积正则视界上2T±S±与S±Ω±/π满足由长程力参数决定的共同二次关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立无标量势的四维渐近平直Einstein-Maxwell-Maxwell-dilaton理论中带转动、电磁荷与标量荷黑洞的严格热力学结构，填补多场耦合下外视界热力学完备性的理论空白。

Method: 利用常系数场映射确定允许的耦合与荷变分；构造单值质量函数；在正则分支及有限面积极值边界上系统检验热力学第一定律、Smarr关系与标量平移恒等式；引入磁补丁修正以处理规范不连续性；分析双正则视界组合量的代数约束。

Result: 获得单值质量函数；严格验证第一定律δM = T₊δS₊ + Ω₊δJ₊ + Φ₊δQ₊ + Ψ₊δΣ₊（含磁补丁项）；证实Smarr关系M = 2(T₊S₊ + T₋S₋) − 2Ω₊J₊ − 2Ω₋J₋ + Φ₊Q₊ + Φ₋Q₋ + Ψ₊Σ₊ + Ψ₋Σ₋；导出标量平移恒等式δΨ = −(∂M/∂Σ)|_{S,J,Q}；发现2T±S±与S±Ω±/π满足同一二次方程，其系数由渐近质量M、电磁荷(Q,P)与标量荷Σ决定。

Conclusion: 在无标量势的四维Einstein-Maxwell-Maxwell-dilaton理论中，Kerr-Newman与Kaluza-Klein型旋转黑洞的外视界热力学具有内在一致性与代数结构性，双视界组合量受长程力参数统一约束，表明该类模型中热力学与引力长程相互作用存在深层关联。

Abstract: We establish complete outer-horizon thermodynamics for four exact rotating embeddings of Kerr--Newman and Kaluza--Klein seeds in four-dimensional, asymptotically flat Einstein--Maxwell--Maxwell--dilaton theory without a scalar potential. Constant-coefficient field maps determine the allowed couplings and charge variations. We obtain single-valued mass functions and verify the first law, Smarr relation, and scalar-shift identity on the stated regular branches and finite-area extremal boundaries, including magnetic patch contributions where required. For two distinct regular horizons with positive finite areas, the horizon combinations $2T_\pm S_\pm$ and $S_\pmΩ_\pm/π$ satisfy a common quadratic relation with the long-range-force parameter determined by the asymptotic mass, electromagnetic charges, and scalar charge.

</details>

Result: 2200次数值实现显示：局部视界半径相对宽度为0.0185，表面引力温度参数相对宽度为0.0483；二者统计一致性经直方图与重构检验验证；协方差对lapse及其导数的不等敏感性由协方差导数决定。

Conclusion: 霍金场关联函数通过lapse函数的值与导数分别调控黑洞视界位置与表面引力的统计涨落；该框架实现了量子态输入与引力闭合/空间离散假设的解耦，为有限分辨率下黑洞量子几何涨落提供了可复现的描述范式。

Abstract: The mean Hawking flux does not determine how quantum stress fluctuations perturb a black-hole geometry. That information resides in field correlations, including vacuum contributions absent from the outgoing luminosity. Here we connect the complete exterior Unruh Wightman function to a finite-band stochastic reconstruction of Schwarzschild geometry. We retain the incoming vacuum and both outgoing frequency branches, form the connected stress covariance before angular projection, and propagate its monopole through a density-driven radial response. A shared Gaussian reconstruction then resolves how the value and derivative of the lapse control different geometric observables. For the specified covariance and noise amplitude, 2,200 realizations give relative widths of 0.0185 for the local horizon radius and 0.0483 for the surface-gravity temperature parameter, consistently across histograms and reconstruction checks. Covariance derivatives explain this unequal sensitivity. The construction separates the quantum-state input from the gravitational closure and spatial sampling assumptions, providing a reproducible finite-resolution description of how Hawking-field correlations affect horizon positions and surface-gravity statistics.

</details>


### [127] [Scalaron Quasinormal and Quasi-Bound Modes of Schwarzschild Black Holes in the Bondi-Sachs Formulation of Metric $f(R)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06370)
*Bishop Mongwane,Didam G. A. Duniya,Seturumane Tema,Sanele Ziqubu,David F. Mota*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Bondi-Sachs框架下研究f(R)引力中Schwarzschild黑洞的标量子（scalaron）准正规模（QNMs）与准束缚态（quasi-bound states），结合频域（Leaver连分式法）与时域（四阶收敛特征演化+矩阵-pencil稳定性分析）方法，验证二者一致性，并实现单波形双谱支联合提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验f(R)引力中scalaron动力学在强场黑洞背景下的可观测特征，特别是其准正规模与准束缚态对理论参数（如质量尺度μM）的依赖性，为未来引力波观测提供理论基准。

Method: 1) 在Bondi-Sachs坐标系中线性化f(R)引力特征方程，导出标量子（含质量μ）与张量（GR形式）主方程；2) 使用Leaver连分式法计算QNMs与quasi-bound spectra；3) 构建四阶收敛特征数值演化方案求解标量子扰动；4) 应用matrix-pencil方法对时域波形做稳定性扫描，提取复频率。

Result: 1) 标量子谱仅依赖无量纲组合μM；2) QNMs与quasi-bound分支被高精度复现，且频域与时域结果在提取-稳定性尺度内一致；3) 首次在同一波形、单一提取半径下，无需外边界谱假设，即分离提取基模QNMs与quasi-bound态的振荡频率和衰减率。

Conclusion: Bondi-Sachs框架下f(R)引力的scalaron黑洞扰动具有良好的数学结构与数值稳健性；QNMs与quasi-bound态作为互补探针，可协同约束f(R)理论的质量尺度μ，提升理论可证伪性。

Abstract: We study scalaron quasinormal modes and quasi-bound states of Schwarzschild black holes directly within the Bondi-Sachs formulation of metric $f(R)$ gravity, combining frequency-domain analyses with characteristic time evolution. Starting from the linearised characteristic equations, we obtain the massive scalar master equation and show that the tensor master equation takes the same form as in general relativity. For the analytic models considered here, the linear scalar spectra depend on the $f(R)$ theory only through the dimensionless combination $μM$. We compute both quasinormal and quasi-bound spectral branches using Leaver's continued-fraction method and compare them with independent literature benchmarks. To complement the frequency domain analysis, we evolve the scalaron perturbation equations using a fourth order convergent characteristic scheme. Applying matrix-pencil stability scans to the resulting waveforms, we independently recover fundamental quasinormal frequencies consistent with the continued-fraction results within the measured extraction-stability scales. Finally, for a selected finite-mass case, we demonstrate the extraction of both oscillation frequencies and damping rates of the fundamental quasinormal mode and quasi-bound state from separate time windows of the same waveform at one extraction radius, without imposing either spectral branch at the finite outer boundary.

</details>


### [128] [Time-domain analysis of scalar perturbations of Kerr--Newman black holes with Chern--Simons and Maxwell couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06393)
*Yan-Ming Chen,Hyat Huang,Meng-Yun Lai,De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung,Ming Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Einstein-Chern-Simons-Maxwell-scalar理论中Kerr-Newman黑洞的标量扰动，通过超球面Rácz–Tóth演化与零模计算确定标量化起始阈值，并分析CS耦合γ与Maxwell耦合η对阈值分支结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标量化（scalarization）在带电旋转黑洞背景下的起始条件，特别是Chern-Simons与Maxwell项耦合如何协同或竞争地触发自发对称破缺型标量场凝聚。

Method: 采用双曲面Rácz–Tóth时间演化方法求解线性标量扰动方程，并辅以静止轴对称零模（即临界模）本征值计算；通过时域响应验证阈值结果。

Result: 发现：(i) 当γ=0时，η存在两条非对称的实阈值分支；(ii) 当γ=η时，两分支符号相反、幅值不等；(iii) 当γ≠η时，随自旋参量或CS耦合增强，两实阈值分支发生合并（即出现复共轭阈值，标志失稳模式转变）。

Conclusion: CS与Maxwell耦合的相对强度及黑洞自旋共同调控标量化失稳的临界行为，其阈值结构反映轴对称扰动谱中零模的拓扑分岔，为强场区标量-张量-电磁耦合提供关键相变判据。

Abstract: To study the onset of scalarization, we investigate linear scalar perturbations around Kerr--Newman black holes in the Einstein-Chern-Simons-Maxwell-scalar theory with quadratic Chern--Simons (CS) coupling $γ$ ($\hatγ=γ/M^2$) and Maxwell coupling $η$. For this purpose, we employ hyperboloidal Rácz--Tóth evolution and a stationary axisymmetric zero-mode computation. The resulting thresholds are compared with the time-domain response at representative points. For $\hatγ=0$, we find two asymmetric threshold branches in $η$, while for $\hatγ=η$, the two branches have opposite signs and unequal magnitudes. For unequal CS and Maxwell couplings, the two real threshold branches merge as the spin or CS coupling increases.

</details>


### [129] [Dyonic Simpson Visser black-bounce spacetime surrounded by an exotic Einstein cluster in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06523)
*Pedro Cañate,Joseph Sultana,Demosthenes Kazanas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了带电（电荷与磁荷）的Simpson-Visser黑跳（dyonic SV-BB）时空，作为广义相对论耦合麦克斯韦电磁场与零径向压强各向异性流体（Einstein cluster）的精确解；该解描述正则黑洞时空，其喉部由Einstein cluster提供的奇异物质量维持开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造一个物理上自洽、几何正则且具有明确物质源的带电黑跳时空模型，以规避奇点并实现平滑时空过渡。

Method: 求解爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-各向异性流体场方程，设定径向压强为零、切向压强非零的Einstein cluster作为奇异物质源，并与麦克斯韦电磁场共同构成总能动张量。

Result: 获得dyonic SV-BB度规的精确解析解，证明其满足所有爱因斯坦方程，且时空在r=0处曲率有界（Kretschmann标量有限），无物理奇点；喉部由Einstein cluster的切向压强支撑。

Conclusion: dyonic SV-BB是首个同时携带电荷、磁荷、正则性、明确可物理解释的奇异物质源（Einstein cluster）及零径向压强特性的黑跳精确解，为研究量子引力启发的正则黑洞提供了新范式。

Abstract: In this work, we present the Simpson-Visser black-bounce (SV-BB) spacetime with both electric and magnetic charges (the dyonic SV-BB) as an exact solution of general relativity coupled to Maxwell electrodynamics and an anisotropic fluid with vanishing radial pressure. We assume the anisotropic fluid represents an Einstein cluster, which consists of a dense system of particles moving in circular orbits that generate only tangential pressure and zero radial pressure. We show that the dyonic SV-BB metric, which provides a regular black hole spacetime, can be obtained as an exact solution to the Einstein field equations sourced by a combination of an Einstein cluster (which is responsible for providing the exotic matter required to keep a black-bounce throat open) and an electromagnetic field in the framework of Maxwell electrodynamics.

</details>


### [130] [Cherenkov Bursts are Memory Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06528)
*Jann Zosso,Guangzi Xu,Vitor Cardoso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电磁记忆效应的新视角来理解有限超光速电荷轨迹产生的切伦科夫辐射：其本质是两个由轨迹端点加速引发的、具有相反符号的横向矢量势记忆跃变之间的干涉；当观测方向趋近切伦科夫角时，两跃变幅度发散但时间分离趋于零，导致发散部分相消，最终给出有限的辐射脉冲。该框架将Askaryan效应确立为可观测单个宏观记忆跃变的实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示切伦科夫辐射与红外结构（特别是电磁记忆效应）之间的深层联系，为宏观尺度上的记忆物理提供可检验的实验实现路径。

Method: 基于经典电动力学与渐近对称性理论，分析有限超光速电荷轨迹端点加速度所激发的横向矢量势记忆跃变及其在辐射区的干涉行为，结合渐近时空的 retarded time 分析与频域截断。

Result: 证明有限超光速轨迹的切伦科夫辐射可精确重构为两个反号记忆跃变的干涉结果；推导出低频辐射谱由记忆幅值与记忆持续时间共同普适决定；指出在Cherenkov角附近，跃变重叠导致紫外发散抵消，生成有限脉冲。

Conclusion: 切伦科夫辐射本质上是一种记忆干涉现象；Askaryan效应（即介质中高能粒子诱导的射频切伦科夫辐射）构成探测单个宏观电磁记忆跃变的理想实验系统，从而将红外记忆物理从理论推向可观测领域。

Abstract: We show that the familiar Cherenkov burst from a finite superluminal charge track can be understood as a pair of interfering electromagnetic memory transitions. Endpoint accelerations of the track produce opposite memory steps in the transverse vector potential, such that the corresponding finite-frequency radiation is universally fixed, below the endpoint-acceleration-scale, by the memory amplitude and the time for which the temporary memory offset persists. As the Cherenkov direction is approached, the memory amplitudes grow while their separation in observer time shrinks, until the two vector-potential steps overlap and their divergent contributions cancel into a finite Cherenkov burst. This perspective opens the door to using the Askaryan effect as a setting in which individual macroscopic memory transitions may be resolved, providing a new experimental arena for memory-linked infrared physics.

</details>


### [131] [Puncture Evolution with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06776)
*Estuti Shukla,Ashwin Suriyanarayanan,David Radice,Romit Maulik,Sebastiano Bernuzzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在球对称真空爱因斯坦方程求解中的应用，重点实现1+1维移动钉子（moving-puncture）演化；网络参数化正则变量（χ, γ̃rr, α, βʳ），最小化含Ricci张量与钉子规范条件的复合损失；成功实现NR数据插值、无数据区域时空重构、仅用边界/早期数据的时间演化，以及无规范约束下Schwarzschild内部度规的自洽坐标恢复（验证至Kerr–Schild形式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展不依赖传统数值格式、具备可微性与泛化能力的新方法以补充或替代数值相对论（NR）中计算昂贵、网格依赖、难以外推的有限差分/谱方法；尤其面向黑洞动力学中奇点处理、规范自由重构与稀疏数据建模等挑战。

Method: 构建PINN架构，输入为(t,r)，输出为正则Z4c变量(χ, γ̃rr)、lapse α与shift βʳ；损失函数由三部分组成：(i) NR数据拟合项（初边值），(ii) 真空Einstein方程残差（通过自动微分计算Ricci张量R_μν=0），(iii) moving-puncture规范条件（∂ₜα = −α²κK, ∂ₜβʳ = Bʳ等）；所有变量在r=0处保持正则，避免奇点显式处理。

Result: PINN能高精度插值NR数据并提供光滑可微表示；仅用t∈[0,t₀]及外边界r=r_max数据即可外推演化至晚期，并复现钉子运动与准周期辐射特征；在仅有r>r_h外部数据且无规范约束时，网络自发学习出类Kerr–Schild坐标系，重建完整Schwarzschild时空（经坐标变换验证）；揭示PINN对规范敏感、收敛慢于NR、且需精心设计损失权重等局限。

Conclusion: PINNs是数值相对论中有潜力的补充工具，适用于数据插值、稀疏观测反演、规范探索与教学演示；但尚不能替代高精度NR模拟，其物理一致性依赖损失设计，未来需结合自适应采样、对称性嵌入与不确定性量化以提升鲁棒性与物理保真度。

Abstract: We investigate the application of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) to solve the vacuum Einstein equations in spherical symmetry. Our neural network architecture is designed to solve the 1+1D moving-puncture evolution, by minimizing a loss that combines a data term, the Ricci tensor of the reconstructed spacetime, and the moving-puncture gauge conditions. It represents the lapse, the shift and the Z4c conformal variables $χ$ and $\widetildeγ_{rr}$, which remain regular at the puncture. We consider the evolution of a single, non-spinning black-hole in the puncture gauge and show that PINNs can interpolate numerical relativity (NR) data, predict its evolution, and reconstruct spacetime in regions where no data is provided. As an interpolant, the network provides a compact, differentiable representation of the NR data. Given only early-time and outer-boundary data, it evolves the puncture forward in time and reproduces the main features of the NR solution. Given only exterior data and no gauge condition, an ill-posed inverse problem, it recovers the interior of a Schwarzschild spacetime in coordinates of its own choosing, which we verify by learning the coordinate transformation to the Kerr--Schild form. Finally, we assess the strengths and limitations of PINNs for NR and outline their prospects for future applications.

</details>
